1659
A variational autoencoder (VAE) model often struggles with "posterior collapse," where it learns to ignore latent variables. This occurs when the model's posterior prediction fails to approximate the true posterior during initial training stages. To address this issue, researchers propose a simple modification to VAE training, which optimizes the inference network based on mutual information between the latent variable and observation. This approach significantly improves the model's performance on held-out likelihood in text and image benchmarks.

Researchers argue that the generalization performance of linear graph embedding methods is not due to dimensionality constraint, but rather the small norm of embedding vectors, supported by both theoretical and empirical evidence.
-----
-----
2264
A new model, called Skip RNN, is introduced to address the challenges in training Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) on long sequences, such as slow inference and vanishing gradients, by learning to skip state updates and reducing the computational graph size.
-----
-----
905
This research paper proposes Super-AND, a comprehensive unsupervised embedding learning approach based on the Anchor Neighbourhood Discovery model. It outperforms existing methods, achieving 89.2% accuracy on CIFAR-10 with Resnet18.
-----
-----
2203
Researchers introduced Hierarchically Clustered Representation Learning (HCRL), a model that simultaneously optimizes representation learning and hierarchical clustering in the embedding space, enabling more meaningful cluster separations and mergers.
-----
-----
912
Researchers propose Genetic Policy Optimization (GPO), a new genetic algorithm for sample-efficient deep policy optimization, combining imitation learning and policy gradient methods to outperform state-of-the-art methods in MuJoCo tasks.
-----
-----
569
Researchers address the issue of mode collapse in Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) by designing discriminators with strong distinguishing power against specific generator classes, enabling GANs to learn distributions with polynomial sample complexity.
-----
-----
2029
This research paper adapts a Q-learning algorithm with UCB (Upper Confidence Bound) exploration bonus to infinite-horizon MDPs with discounted rewards, achieving a sample complexity of exploration bounded by $\tilde{O}({\frac{SA}{\epsilon^2(1-\gamma)^7}})$.
-----
-----
410
This paper adapts the out-of-distribution detection method ODIN to sentence classification and word tagging tasks, using its scores as a confidence measure for both in- and out-of-distribution datasets.
-----
-----
351
A new model, Information Maximising Autoencoder (InfoAE), is proposed for learning disentangled representations from unlabelled data in an unsupervised manner, achieving 98.9% accuracy on the MNIST dataset.
-----
-----
1231
This research proposes the LDMGAN framework to address the issue of mode collapse in Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) by introducing a Latent Distribution Matching constraint and a regularized AutoEncoder to enhance GAN stability and diversity.
-----
-----
989
A novel algorithm, Deep Temporal Clustering (DTC), is proposed to tackle unsupervised learning of timeseries data by integrating dimensionality reduction and clustering into a single end-to-end framework, outperforming traditional methods across various domains.
-----
-----
1585
Researchers developed a method to train Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) to optimize user interactions, such as aesthetic ratings, by modeling human behavior from a small set of interactions and incorporating it as an auxiliary loss function.
-----
-----
417
This paper introduces Generative Latent Optimization (GLO), a framework for training deep convolutional generators without discriminators, to disentangle the factors contributing to the success of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs).
-----
-----
2614
This paper proposes a data imputation framework combining variational autoencoders and normalizing flows, allowing for mapping partial data to a multi-modal latent distribution, enabling stochastic imputation with promising results on the MNIST dataset.
-----
-----
920
This paper investigates the connection between Hidden Markov Models (HMMs) and Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), showing that HMMs are a special case of RNNs.
-----
-----
279
Researchers propose a Proximal-Type Stochastic Gradient Descent (Prox-SGD) algorithm for training neural networks with specific structures, incorporating non-smooth regularization and constraints, and demonstrate its convergence and effectiveness in training sparse and binary neural networks.
-----
-----
2288
This research explores the relationship between Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and Variational AutoEncoders (VAEs) by demonstrating that an optimal transport GAN with entropy regularization can be viewed as a generative model maximizing a lower-bound on average sample likelihoods.
-----
-----
2996
A new autoencoding model is proposed, combining the strengths of variational autoencoders (VAE) and generative adversarial networks (GAN). It optimizes λ-Jeffreys divergence to balance mode-seeking and mode-covering, achieving state-of-the-art results on CIFAR-10 and TinyImageNet datasets.
-----
-----
991
A novel algorithm, Deep Temporal Clustering (DTC), is proposed for unsupervised learning of timeseries data. It integrates dimensionality reduction and temporal clustering into a single framework, outperforming traditional methods across various domains.
-----
-----
1858
Researchers propose using the acceptance rate of the Metropolis-Hastings algorithm as a universal objective for learning to sample from target distributions, unifying MCMC, GANs, and variational inference methods.
